```
    35→## What Was Built
    36→
    37→`DocToLoraHypernetwork` in `libs/model-training/src/model_training/hypernetwork.py` — a Perceiver architecture that cross-attends over token embeddings with learned latents to produce PEFT-compatible LoRA weight matrices for `q_proj` and `v_proj` across all transformer layers.
    38→
    39→Key components:
    40→- **Lazy proxy pattern**: `DocToLoraHypernetwork` is a `_LazyHypernetworkProxy` at module level; the real `nn.Module` subclass is built and cached on first instantiation. This keeps the module importable without `torch` installed (INFRA-05 compliance).
    41→- **Perceiver architecture**: learned token embedding (not the 7B base model's), 32 latent vectors (256 dim), cross-attention (latents attend over token embeddings), self-attention stack (depth=4, heads=8), linear weight head projecting to all LoRA parameters.
    42→- **forward() output**: PEFT state_dict keys following `base_model.model.model.layers.{i}.self_attn.{module}.lora_{A|B}.weight` with lora_A shape (rank, hidden_dim) and lora_B shape (hidden_dim, rank). Returns first batch element.
    43→- **save_hypernetwork_adapter()**: writes `adapter_model.safetensors` via deferred `safetensors.torch.save_file` import and `adapter_config.json` with correct PEFT fields. `modules_to_save=None` prevents vLLM rejection.
    44→
    45→## Tasks Completed
    46→

    66→## Accomplishments
    67→
    68→- Replaced all 4 NotImplementedError stubs with working SQLModel CRUD operations
    69→- Wired WAL mode via SQLAlchemy `event.listens_for` hook registered before `create_all`
    70→- Changed constructor to require an `Engine` parameter (engine stored as `self._engine`)
    71→- All 11 tests pass; mypy strict and ruff clean with zero errors
    72→- Updated test suite from red-phase stubs to full green-phase behavioral tests
    73→
    74→## Task Commits
    75→
    76→Each task was committed atomically:
    77→
    78→1. **Task 1: Implement AdapterRegistry with constructor and 4 CRUD methods** - `a900149` (feat)
    79→
    80→**Plan metadata:** TBD (docs: complete plan)
    81→
    82→_Note: TDD task committed in single green-phase commit after implementation._
    83→
    84→## Files Created/Modified
    85→
    86→- `libs/adapter-registry/src/adapter_registry/registry.py` - Full implementation replacing all NotImplementedError stubs with SQLModel CRUD
    87→- `libs/adapter-registry/tests/test_registry.py` - 11 behavioral tests covering constructor, store, retrieve_by_id, query_by_task_type, and list_all
    88→
    89→## Decisions Made
    90→
    91→- **Engine from sqlalchemy.engine:** SQLModel does not re-export `Engine`; must import from `sqlalchemy.engine` directly. Plan listed `from sqlmodel import Engine` which fails — corrected to `from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine`.
    92→- **expire_on_commit=False + session.expunge():** All read methods use this pattern to prevent `DetachedInstanceError` when callers access attributes after session close. The `store()` method also expunges the caller's record object to prevent the same issue.
    93→- **# noqa: E712:** Added to `is_archived == False` comparison per plan instructions to suppress ruff E712 (comparison to False).
    94→
    95→## Deviations from Plan
    96→
    97→### Auto-fixed Issues
    98→
    99→**1. [Rule 1 - Bug] Fixed incorrect Engine import from sqlmodel**
   100→- **Found during:** Task 1 (implementation)
   101→- **Issue:** Plan specified `from sqlmodel import Engine` but SQLModel does not re-export Engine; import fails with `ImportError: cannot import name 'Engine' from 'sqlmodel'`
   102→- **Fix:** Changed to `from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine` in both registry.py and test_registry.py
   103→- **Files modified:** `libs/adapter-registry/src/adapter_registry/registry.py`, `libs/adapter-registry/tests/test_registry.py`
   104→- **Verification:** Import succeeds, mypy passes
   105→- **Committed in:** a900149 (Task 1 commit)
   106→
   107→**2. [Rule 1 - Bug] Fixed DetachedInstanceError on returned SQLModel records**
   108→- **Found during:** Task 1 (test execution)
   109→- **Issue:** `retrieve_by_id()`, `query_by_task_type()`, and `list_all()` returned ORM objects that became detached after session close, causing `DetachedInstanceError` when callers accessed any attribute. `store()` also left the caller's record object in an expired/detached state.
   110→- **Fix:** Added `expire_on_commit=False` to all Session constructors and explicit `session.expunge()` calls before returning records. This detaches objects cleanly with all attributes loaded in memory.
   111→- **Files modified:** `libs/adapter-registry/src/adapter_registry/registry.py`
   112→- **Verification:** All 11 tests pass including `test_store_persists_record` and `test_retrieve_by_id_returns_matching_record` which access attributes after session close
   113→- **Committed in:** a900149 (Task 1 commit)
   114→
   115→---
   116→
   117→**Total deviations:** 2 auto-fixed (2 bugs)
   118→**Impact on plan:** Both fixes required for correctness. Engine import is a factual error in plan; session expunge is a necessary SQLAlchemy pattern when using per-method sessions. No scope creep.

    78→- InferenceProvider ABC with 4 async abstract methods (generate, load_adapter, unload_adapter, list_adapters) providing a provider-agnostic contract for the agent loop
    79→- VLLMProvider fully implementing all 4 methods: AsyncOpenAI for generation, httpx for vLLM proprietary LoRA endpoints, internal adapter tracking set for reliability
    80→- OllamaProvider implementing generate via Ollama's OpenAI-compat endpoint, raising UnsupportedOperationError for adapter ops with descriptive messages
    81→- 23 tests total (6 + 9 + 8) covering ABC constraints, dataclass construction, generate routing, adapter lifecycle, async verification

    89→3. **Task 3: Implement OllamaProvider with UnsupportedOperationError for adapters** - `c70ff90` (feat)

   115→- **Found during:** Task 2 (VLLMProvider implementation)
   116→- **Issue:** The inference lib's own pyproject.toml has `[tool.pytest.ini_options]` which overrides the root config. It was missing `asyncio_mode = "auto"`, so pytest ran in strict mode and all async test methods failed with "async def functions are not natively supported"
   117→- **Fix:** Added `asyncio_mode = "auto"` to `[tool.pytest.ini_options]` in `libs/inference/pyproject.toml` and added `pytest-asyncio>=1.0.0` to dev dependencies
   118→- **Files modified:** `libs/inference/pyproject.toml`

   120→- **Committed in:** `1613667` (Task 2 commit)

    93→## Decisions Made
    94→
    95→- Used `os.environ.get()` instead of `os.getenv()` in factory.py — both have the same mypy narrowing limitation but explicit `str` type annotation on the result resolves mypy's union-attr errors cleanly
    96→- Env var reads placed inside `get_provider()` function body (not at module level) — module-level reads are captured at import time before monkeypatch.setenv() takes effect; function-body reads re-evaluate on each call
    97→- `_clear_cache()` helper retained in factory.py alongside the conftest autouse fixture as belt-and-suspenders isolation
    98→
    99→## Deviations from Plan
   100→
   101→### Auto-fixed Issues
   102→
   103→**1. [Rule 1 - Bug] Env var read at module level prevented monkeypatch testability**
   104→- **Found during:** Task 1 (factory TDD GREEN phase)
   105→- **Issue:** `INFERENCE_PROVIDER = os.getenv(...)` at module level is evaluated at import time; monkeypatch.setenv() after import has no effect. Test `test_get_provider_uses_env_var_default` failed.
   106→- **Fix:** Moved env var reads inside `get_provider()` function body using `os.environ.get(key, default)` with explicit type annotations
   107→- **Files modified:** `libs/inference/src/inference/factory.py`
   108→- **Verification:** 9/9 factory tests pass including env var default tests
   109→- **Committed in:** `f482162` (Task 2 commit with mypy fix)
   110→
   111→**2. [Rule 1 - Bug] mypy type errors from os.getenv() return type**
   112→- **Found during:** Task 2 verification (mypy run)
   113→- **Issue:** `os.getenv(key, str_default)` returns `str | None` in mypy's type stubs even when a str default is provided; caused union-attr and dict index type errors in factory.py
   114→- **Fix:** Used `os.environ.get()` with explicit `str` variable annotations and extracted resolved values to typed locals before use
   115→- **Files modified:** `libs/inference/src/inference/factory.py`
   116→- **Verification:** `uv run mypy src/` reports "Success: no issues found in 6 source files"
   117→- **Committed in:** `f482162`

    66→
    67→## Performance

    78→- Implemented `get_training_config()` with all locked hyperparameters and `validate_config()` with range checks
    79→- Created `train_qlora()` orchestrator: load_trajectory -> format_for_sft -> Dataset.from_list -> BitsAndBytesConfig -> SFTTrainer -> save_model
    80→- Created `train_and_register()` wrapping full pipeline with file hash/size computation and AdapterRegistry.store()
    81→- All 5 GPU dep packages declared in model-training pyproject.toml; bitsandbytes/trl mypy overrides added

    89→2. **Task 2: train_qlora orchestrator, __init__.py, trainer tests** - `2e3a1e2` (feat)

    93→## Files Created/Modified
    94→
    95→- `libs/model-training/src/model_training/peft_utils.py` - build_qlora_config and apply_lora_adapter with deferred peft imports
    96→- `libs/model-training/src/model_training/config.py` - get_training_config and validate_config implementations
    97→- `libs/model-training/src/model_training/trainer.py` - NEW: train_qlora and train_and_register orchestrators
    98→- `libs/model-training/src/model_training/__init__.py` - Updated comment documenting GPU deferred import pattern
    99→- `libs/model-training/pyproject.toml` - Added peft, bitsandbytes, transformers, trl, datasets dependencies
   100→- `pyproject.toml` - Added bitsandbytes and trl mypy override blocks
   101→- `libs/model-training/tests/test_peft_utils.py` - Green-phase tests with sys.modules injection
   102→- `libs/model-training/tests/test_config.py` - Green-phase tests covering all validation paths
   103→- `libs/model-training/tests/test_trainer.py` - NEW: importability, FileNotFoundError, ValueError tests
   104→
   105→## Decisions Made
   106→
   107→- **bfloat16 in BitsAndBytesConfig not LoraConfig**: bfloat16 compute dtype is about the quantized forward pass, not the LoRA layer geometry. Setting it in LoraConfig is wrong.
   108→- **No modules_to_save in LoraConfig**: Would cause adapter to include embed_tokens/lm_head in the saved PEFT artifact, breaking vLLM adapter loading which expects only q_proj/v_proj weights.
   109→- **sys.modules injection for GPU mock tests**: `unittest.mock.patch("peft.get_peft_model")` fails when `peft` is not installed. Injecting a `ModuleType` fake into `sys.modules` before calling the deferred-import function is the correct CPU-CI pattern.
   110→- **RUNE_* env vars read inside function bodies**: Consistent with Phase 19/20 factory.py and trajectory.py patterns — enables monkeypatch.setenv() in tests.
   111→

   113→
   114→### Auto-fixed Issues
   115→
   116→**1. [Rule 1 - Bug] Fixed test_apply_lora_adapter_calls_get_peft_model using sys.modules injection**

   118→- **Issue:** `unittest.mock.patch("peft.get_peft_model")` raises `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'peft'` in CPU CI because `patch` attempts to resolve the module before substituting it
   119→- **Fix:** Replaced patch() with direct `sys.modules["peft"] = fake_peft` injection before calling `apply_lora_adapter`, with cleanup in finally block
   120→- **Files modified:** `libs/model-training/tests/test_peft_utils.py`
   121→- **Verification:** Test passes without peft installed
   122→- **Committed in:** `8a60a22` (Task 1 commit)

   127→- **Fix:** Reordered deferred imports to stdlib-first (`torch`), then third-party (`datasets`, `transformers`, `trl`), then first-party. Shortened comment line.

    29→---
    30→
    31→# Phase 22 Plan 01: DocToLoraHypernetwork Summary
    32→
    33→**One-liner:** Perceiver-based hypernetwork generating rank-8 LoRA adapters from token IDs in a single forward pass via lazy nn.Module proxy pattern for CPU-only importability.
    34→
    35→## What Was Built
    36→
    37→`DocToLoraHypernetwork` in `libs/model-training/src/model_training/hypernetwork.py` — a Perceiver architecture that cross-attends over token embeddings with learned latents to produce PEFT-compatible LoRA weight matrices for `q_proj` and `v_proj` across all transformer layers.
    38→
    39→Key components:
    40→- **Lazy proxy pattern**: `DocToLoraHypernetwork` is a `_LazyHypernetworkProxy` at module level; the real `nn.Module` subclass is built and cached on first instantiation. This keeps the module importable without `torch` installed (INFRA-05 compliance).
    41→- **Perceiver architecture**: learned token embedding (not the 7B base model's), 32 latent vectors (256 dim), cross-attention (latents attend over token embeddings), self-attention stack (depth=4, heads=8), linear weight head projecting to all LoRA parameters.
    42→- **forward() output**: PEFT state_dict keys following `base_model.model.model.layers.{i}.self_attn.{module}.lora_{A|B}.weight` with lora_A shape (rank, hidden_dim) and lora_B shape (hidden_dim, rank). Returns first batch element.
    43→- **save_hypernetwork_adapter()**: writes `adapter_model.safetensors` via deferred `safetensors.torch.save_file` import and `adapter_config.json` with correct PEFT fields. `modules_to_save=None` prevents vLLM rejection.
    44→
    45→## Tasks Completed
    46→
    47→| Task | Name | Commit | Files |
    48→|------|------|--------|-------|
    49→| RED | Failing tests for DocToLoraHypernetwork | 373f210 | test_hypernetwork.py (290 lines) |
    50→| GREEN | Implementation + __init__.py update | 4f91ad3 | hypernetwork.py (268 lines), __init__.py, test fixes |
    51→

    53→
    54→- `uv run python -c "from model_training.hypernetwork import DocToLoraHypernetwork"` — PASSED (CPU-only, no torch)
    55→- `uv run pytest libs/model-training/tests/test_hypernetwork.py -x -v` — 7/7 PASSED
    56→- `uv run ruff check libs/model-training/src/model_training/hypernetwork.py` — PASSED
    57→- `uv run mypy libs/model-training/src/model_training/hypernetwork.py` — PASSED (no issues)
    58→
    59→## Deviations from Plan
    60→
    61→### Auto-fixed Issues
    62→
    63→**1. [Rule 1 - Bug] Fixed test construction OOM for full-size model**
    64→- **Found during:** GREEN phase (first test run)
    65→- **Issue:** `test_hypernetwork_latents_shape` created `DocToLoraHypernetwork(input_dim=32000)` with default `hidden_dim=4096` and 28 layers, making `weight_head` a `8192 x 3,670,016` linear layer — hung indefinitely on CPU.
    66→- **Fix:** Tests that exercise forward/construction use `hidden_dim=32, num_layers=1-2` to stay fast on CPU. Latents shape test keeps `num_latents=32, latent_dim=256` (the defaults being tested) but uses small `hidden_dim`.
    67→- **Files modified:** `libs/model-training/tests/test_hypernetwork.py`
    68→- **Commit:** 4f91ad3

     1→# Deployment stack: API, UI, Postgres, MLflow, Pub/Sub emulator, lora-server.
     2→# Run from repo root: docker-compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up --build
     3→#
     4→# Port layout:
     5→#   api-service  : host 8000 -> container 8000

     8→services:
     9→  api:
    10→    build:

     1→"""FastAPI application for the training service."""
     2→
     3→import logging
     4→from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
     5→
     6→from fastapi import FastAPI
     7→
     8→from training_svc.routers.training import router as training_router
     9→from training_svc.storage import create_db_and_tables
    10→
    11→# Setup basic logging
    12→logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
    13→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    14→
    15→
    16→@asynccontextmanager
    17→async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    18→    """Manage the lifecycle of the FastAPI application."""
    19→    logger.info("Starting up training service...")
    20→    create_db_and_tables()
    21→    logger.info("Database initialized successfully")
    22→    yield
    23→    logger.info("Shutdown complete")
    24→
    25→
    26→app = FastAPI(lifespan=lifespan)
    27→app.include_router(training_router)
    28→
    29→
    30→@app.get("/health")
    31→async def health_check():
    32→    """Liveness probe - is the service running?"""
    33→    return {"status": "healthy", "service": "training-svc"}

     1→"""Training router — POST /train/lora, POST /train/hypernetwork, GET /jobs/{id}."""
     2→
     3→import os
     4→import uuid
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from fastapi import APIRouter, BackgroundTasks, HTTPException
     8→from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
     9→
    10→from training_svc.jobs import JOB_STORE, JobStatus
    11→from training_svc.schemas import (
    12→    HypernetworkTrainingRequest,
    13→    LoraTrainingRequest,
    14→)
    15→
    16→router = APIRouter(tags=["training"])
    17→
    18→
    19→def _run_training_job(
    20→    job_id: str,
    21→    session_id: str,
    22→    adapter_id: str,
    23→    task_type: str,
    24→    rank: int,
    25→    epochs: int,
    26→    learning_rate: float,
    27→) -> None:
    28→    """Background worker: runs train_and_register and updates JOB_STORE.
    29→
    30→    Import is deferred inside the function body per INFRA-05 (GPU deps
    31→    should not be imported at service startup in non-GPU environments).
    32→    """
    33→    JOB_STORE[job_id].status = "running"
    34→    try:
    35→        from model_training.trainer import train_and_register  # noqa: PLC0415
    36→
    37→        train_and_register(
    38→            session_id=session_id,
    39→            adapter_id=adapter_id,
    40→            task_type=task_type,
    41→            rank=rank,
    42→            epochs=epochs,
    43→            learning_rate=learning_rate,
    44→        )
    45→        JOB_STORE[job_id].status = "completed"
    46→    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
    47→        JOB_STORE[job_id].status = "failed"
    48→        JOB_STORE[job_id].error = str(e)
    49→
    50→
    51→@router.post("/train/lora")
    52→async def train_lora(
    53→    request: LoraTrainingRequest,
    54→    background_tasks: BackgroundTasks,
    55→) -> JSONResponse:
    56→    """Dispatch a QLoRA training job as a background task.
    57→
    58→    Args:
    59→        request: LoRA training parameters — session_id is required.
    60→        background_tasks: FastAPI background task runner.
    61→
    62→    Returns:
    63→        JSONResponse with job_id and status="queued".
    64→
    65→    Example:
    66→        >>> body = {"session_id": "s-1", "task_type": "code-gen", "epochs": 3}
    67→        >>> response = client.post("/train/lora", json=body)
    68→        >>> response.status_code
    69→        200
    70→        >>> response.json()["status"]
    71→        'queued'
    72→    """
    73→    job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    74→    adapter_id = request.adapter_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
    75→
    76→    JOB_STORE[job_id] = JobStatus(
    77→        job_id=job_id,
    78→        status="queued",
    79→        adapter_id=adapter_id,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→    background_tasks.add_task(
    83→        _run_training_job,
    84→        job_id,
    85→        request.session_id,
    86→        adapter_id,
    87→        request.task_type,
    88→        request.rank,
    89→        request.epochs,
    90→        request.learning_rate,
    91→    )
    92→
    93→    return JSONResponse(content={"job_id": job_id, "status": "queued"}, status_code=200)
    94→
    95→
    96→def _run_hypernetwork_job(job_id: str, trajectory_id: str, task_type: str) -> None:
    97→    """Background worker: runs DocToLoraHypernetwork forward pass and saves adapter.
    98→
    99→    Import is deferred inside the function body per INFRA-05 (GPU deps
   100→    should not be imported at service startup in non-GPU environments).
   101→
   102→    Args:
   103→        job_id: Unique job identifier used to update JOB_STORE.
   104→        trajectory_id: Trajectory ID to load and tokenize.
   105→        task_type: Task type string (stored for context, not used in forward pass).
   106→    """
   107→    JOB_STORE[job_id].status = "running"
   108→    try:
   109→        import torch  # noqa: PLC0415
   110→        from model_training.hypernetwork import (  # noqa: PLC0415
   111→            DocToLoraHypernetwork,
   112→            save_hypernetwork_adapter,
   113→        )
   114→        from model_training.trajectory import (  # noqa: PLC0415
   115→            format_for_sft,
   116→            load_trajectory,
   117→        )
   118→        from transformers import AutoTokenizer  # noqa: PLC0415
   119→
   120→        # Load and tokenize trajectory
   121→        trajectory = load_trajectory(trajectory_id)
   122→        messages = format_for_sft(trajectory)
   123→        # Concatenate messages into a single text for tokenization
   124→        text = " ".join(m["content"] for m in messages)
   125→
   126→        # Resolve env vars inside function body for testability
   127→        base_model_id = os.environ.get(
   128→            "RUNE_BASE_MODEL", "Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct"
   129→        )
   130→        hypernetwork_weights_path = os.environ.get(
   131→            "RUNE_HYPERNETWORK_WEIGHTS_PATH",
   132→            str(Path.home() / ".rune" / "hypernetwork.pt"),
   133→        )
   134→        adapter_base = os.environ.get("RUNE_ADAPTER_DIR")
   135→
   136→        # Tokenize using base model tokenizer (lightweight, no 7B model loading)
   137→        tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(base_model_id)
   138→        token_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, return_tensors="pt")
   139→
   140→        # Load hypernetwork and generate adapter
   141→        hypernetwork = DocToLoraHypernetwork(
   142→            input_dim=tokenizer.vocab_size,
   143→        )
   144→        hypernetwork.load_state_dict(
   145→            torch.load(hypernetwork_weights_path, map_location="cpu")
   146→        )
   147→        hypernetwork.eval()
   148→
   149→        with torch.no_grad():
   150→            weights = hypernetwork(token_ids)
   151→
   152→        # Save adapter
   153→        adapter_id = JOB_STORE[job_id].adapter_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
   154→        if adapter_base:
   155→            adapter_dir = str(Path(adapter_base) / adapter_id)
   156→        else:
   157→            adapter_dir = str(Path.home() / ".rune" / "adapters" / adapter_id)
   158→
   159→        save_hypernetwork_adapter(
   160→            weights=weights,
   161→            output_dir=adapter_dir,
   162→            base_model_id=base_model_id,
   163→        )
   164→
   165→        JOB_STORE[job_id].status = "completed"
   166→        JOB_STORE[job_id].adapter_id = adapter_id
   167→    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
   168→        JOB_STORE[job_id].status = "failed"
   169→        JOB_STORE[job_id].error = str(e)
   170→
   171→
   172→@router.post("/train/hypernetwork")
   173→async def train_hypernetwork(
   174→    request: HypernetworkTrainingRequest,
   175→    background_tasks: BackgroundTasks,
   176→) -> JSONResponse:
   177→    """Dispatch a hypernetwork adapter generation job as a background task.
   178→
   179→    Accepts a trajectory, runs it through the pre-trained hypernetwork in a
   180→    single forward pass, saves the adapter in PEFT format, and returns a
   181→    job_id for status polling via GET /jobs/{job_id}.
   182→
   183→    Args:
   184→        request: Hypernetwork training parameters including task_type and
   185→            trajectory_ids (uses first trajectory_id).
   186→        background_tasks: FastAPI background task runner.
   187→
   188→    Returns:
   189→        JSONResponse with job_id and status="queued".
   190→
   191→    Example:
   192→        >>> body = {"task_type": "gen", "trajectory_ids": ["t-1"]}
   193→        >>> response = client.post("/train/hypernetwork", json=body)
   194→        >>> response.status_code
   195→        200
   196→        >>> response.json()["status"]
   197→        'queued'
   198→    """
   199→    job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
   200→
   201→    JOB_STORE[job_id] = JobStatus(
   202→        job_id=job_id,
   203→        status="queued",
   204→    )
   205→
   206→    background_tasks.add_task(
   207→        _run_hypernetwork_job,
   208→        job_id,
   209→        request.trajectory_ids[0],
   210→        request.task_type,
   211→    )
   212→
   213→    return JSONResponse(content={"job_id": job_id, "status": "queued"}, status_code=200)
   214→
   215→
   216→@router.get("/jobs/{job_id}")
   217→async def get_job_status(job_id: str) -> JSONResponse:
   218→    """Get training job status.
   219→
   220→    Args:
   221→        job_id: Unique identifier for the training job.
   222→
   223→    Returns:
   224→        JSONResponse with job_id, status, adapter_id, and optional error.
   225→
   226→    Raises:
   227→        HTTPException: 404 if job_id not found.
   228→
   229→    Example:
   230→        >>> response = client.get("/jobs/job-123")
   231→        >>> response.status_code
   232→        404
   233→    """
   234→    job = JOB_STORE.get(job_id)
   235→    if job is None:
   236→        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Job {job_id} not found")
   237→
   238→    return JSONResponse(
   239→        content={
   240→            "job_id": job.job_id,
   241→            "status": job.status,
   242→            "adapter_id": job.adapter_id,
   243→            "error": job.error,
   244→        },
   245→        status_code=200,
   246→    )
   247→

```
     1→"""DocToLoraHypernetwork: Perceiver-based instant LoRA adapter generation.
     2→
     3→Generates rank-8 LoRA adapter weights from token IDs in a single forward pass.
     4→Distinct from the QLoRA gradient-descent path (Phase 21) — this produces adapters
     5→in <1s by cross-attending over token embeddings with learned latents.
     6→
     7→IMPORTANT: All GPU imports (torch, safetensors) are deferred inside function/method
     8→bodies per INFRA-05 pattern — this module is importable in CPU-only CI.
     9→
    10→Usage:
    11→    from model_training.hypernetwork import (
    12→        DocToLoraHypernetwork,
    13→        save_hypernetwork_adapter,
    14→    )
    15→
    16→    model = DocToLoraHypernetwork(input_dim=32000)
    17→    weights = model(token_ids)
    18→    save_hypernetwork_adapter(weights, "/tmp/adapter", "Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B")
    19→"""
    20→
    21→from __future__ import annotations
    22→
    23→import json
    24→from pathlib import Path
    25→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Sequence
    26→
    27→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    28→    import torch
    29→
    30→
    31→def _build_hypernetwork_class() -> type:
    32→    """Build and return DocToLoraHypernetwork as a real nn.Module subclass.
    33→
    34→    This function is called the first time DocToLoraHypernetwork is instantiated
    35→    so that torch.nn.Module is only imported when needed (INFRA-05 pattern).
    36→    """
    37→    import torch  # noqa: PLC0415
    38→    import torch.nn as nn  # noqa: PLC0415
    39→
    40→    class _DocToLoraHypernetwork(nn.Module):
    41→        """Perceiver-based hypernetwork that generates LoRA adapter weights.
    42→
    43→        Given a sequence of token IDs (from a document/trajectory), produces a
    44→        PEFT-compatible state_dict with lora_A and lora_B matrices for every
    45→        target_module across all transformer layers — in a single forward pass.
    46→
    47→        Architecture:
    48→        - Lightweight learned token embedding (not the 7B base model's embedding)
    49→        - Learnable latent array (num_latents x latent_dim)
    50→        - Cross-attention: latents attend over token embeddings
    51→        - Self-attention stack: process latents
    52→        - Linear head: project to all LoRA weight parameters
    53→
    54→        Args:
    55→            input_dim: Vocabulary size for the hypernetwork's own token embedding.
    56→            num_latents: Number of learnable latent vectors. Default: 32.
    57→            latent_dim: Dimensionality of each latent vector. Default: 256.
    58→            depth: Number of self-attention layers over latents. Default: 4.
    59→            heads: Number of attention heads. Default: 8.
    60→            rank: LoRA rank for generated adapters. Default: 8.
    61→            target_modules: LoRA target module names. Default: ("q_proj", "v_proj").
    62→            num_layers: Number of transformer layers in target model. Default: 28.
    63→            hidden_dim: Hidden dim of q_proj/v_proj in target model. Default: 4096.
    64→        """
    65→
    66→        def __init__(
    67→            self,
    68→            input_dim: int,
    69→            num_latents: int = 32,
    70→            latent_dim: int = 256,
    71→            depth: int = 4,
    72→            heads: int = 8,
    73→            rank: int = 8,
    74→            target_modules: Sequence[str] = ("q_proj", "v_proj"),
    75→            num_layers: int = 28,
    76→            hidden_dim: int = 4096,
    77→        ) -> None:
    78→            super().__init__()
    79→
    80→            self.rank = rank
    81→            self.hidden_dim = hidden_dim
    82→            self.target_modules: list[str] = list(target_modules)
    83→            self.num_layers = num_layers
    84→            self.num_latents = num_latents
    85→            self.latent_dim = latent_dim
    86→
    87→            # Hypernetwork's own lightweight embedding — does NOT load the 7B model
    88→            self.token_embedding = nn.Embedding(input_dim, latent_dim)
    89→
    90→            # Learnable latent array: shape (num_latents, latent_dim)
    91→            self.latents = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(num_latents, latent_dim))
    92→
    93→            # Cross-attention: latents (Q) attend over token embeddings (K, V)
    94→            self.cross_attend = nn.MultiheadAttention(
    95→                latent_dim, heads, batch_first=True
    96→            )
    97→
    98→            # Self-attention stack to process latents
    99→            encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(
   100→                latent_dim, heads, batch_first=True
   101→            )
   102→            self.self_attend = nn.TransformerEncoder(
   103→                encoder_layer, num_layers=depth
   104→            )
   105→
   106→            # Project flattened latents to all LoRA weight parameters.
   107→            # Each (layer, module) needs:
   108→            #   lora_A: rank * hidden_dim params
   109→            #   lora_B: hidden_dim * rank params
   110→            total_params = (
   111→                len(target_modules) * num_layers * 2 * rank * hidden_dim
   112→            )
   113→            self.weight_head = nn.Linear(latent_dim * num_latents, total_params)
   114→
   115→        def forward(
   116→            self, token_ids: torch.Tensor
   117→        ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
   118→            """Generate PEFT-compatible LoRA adapter weights from token IDs.
   119→
   120→            Args:
   121→                token_ids: Integer token ID tensor of shape (batch, seq_len).
   122→
   123→            Returns:
   124→                Dict mapping PEFT state_dict keys to weight tensors.
   125→                Keys follow:
   126→                  base_model.model.model.layers.{i}.self_attn.{module}.lora_{A|B}.weight
   127→                Returns first batch element (one adapter per trajectory).
   128→            """
   129→            batch_size = token_ids.shape[0]
   130→
   131→            # Embed token IDs with hypernetwork's own lightweight embedding
   132→            x = self.token_embedding(token_ids)  # (batch, seq_len, latent_dim)
   133→
   134→            # Expand latents for batch dimension
   135→            latents = self.latents.unsqueeze(0).expand(
   136→                batch_size, -1, -1
   137→            )  # (batch, num_latents, latent_dim)
   138→
   139→            # Cross-attention: latents attend over token embeddings
   140→            latents, _ = self.cross_attend(
   141→                latents, x, x
   142→            )  # (batch, num_latents, latent_dim)
   143→
   144→            # Self-attention stack over latents
   145→            latents = self.self_attend(latents)  # (batch, num_latents, latent_dim)
   146→
   147→            # Flatten latents
   148→            latents = latents.reshape(
   149→                batch_size, -1
   150→            )  # (batch, num_latents * latent_dim)
   151→
   152→            # Project to all LoRA weight parameters
   153→            weights = self.weight_head(latents)  # (batch, total_weight_params)
   154→
   155→            # Reshape into PEFT state_dict for first batch element (index 0)
   156→            state_dict: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
   157→            offset = 0
   158→            a_size = self.rank * self.hidden_dim
   159→            b_size = self.hidden_dim * self.rank
   160→
   161→            for i in range(self.num_layers):
   162→                for module in self.target_modules:
   163→                    # lora_A: shape (rank, hidden_dim)
   164→                    key_a = (
   165→                        f"base_model.model.model.layers.{i}"
   166→                        f".self_attn.{module}.lora_A.weight"
   167→                    )
   168→                    state_dict[key_a] = weights[
   169→                        0, offset : offset + a_size
   170→                    ].reshape(self.rank, self.hidden_dim)
   171→                    offset += a_size
   172→
   173→                    # lora_B: shape (hidden_dim, rank)
   174→                    key_b = (
   175→                        f"base_model.model.model.layers.{i}"
   176→                        f".self_attn.{module}.lora_B.weight"
   177→                    )
   178→                    state_dict[key_b] = weights[
   179→                        0, offset : offset + b_size
   180→                    ].reshape(self.hidden_dim, self.rank)
   181→                    offset += b_size
   182→
   183→            return state_dict
   184→
   185→    return _DocToLoraHypernetwork
   186→
   187→
   188→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   189→# DocToLoraHypernetwork: lazy proxy that builds the real nn.Module class on
   190→# first instantiation. This keeps the module importable without torch.
   191→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   192→
   193→class _LazyHypernetworkProxy:
   194→    """Lazy proxy for DocToLoraHypernetwork.
   195→
   196→    Acts as a callable that creates a real nn.Module instance on first call.
   197→    The class is rebuilt (and cached) on first instantiation so that torch is
   198→    only imported when actually needed (INFRA-05 pattern).
   199→    """
   200→
   201→    _real_class: type | None = None
   202→
   203→    def __call__(self, *args: object, **kwargs: object) -> object:
   204→        if self._real_class is None:
   205→            self.__class__._real_class = _build_hypernetwork_class()
   206→        return self._real_class(*args, **kwargs)  # type: ignore[misc]
   207→
   208→    def __instancecheck__(self, instance: object) -> bool:
   209→        if self._real_class is None:
   210→            return False
   211→        return isinstance(instance, self._real_class)
   212→
   213→
   214→DocToLoraHypernetwork: _LazyHypernetworkProxy = _LazyHypernetworkProxy()
   215→
   216→
   217→def save_hypernetwork_adapter(
   218→    weights: dict[str, "torch.Tensor"],
   219→    output_dir: str,
   220→    base_model_id: str,
   221→    rank: int = 8,
   222→    target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
   223→) -> None:
   224→    """Serialize hypernetwork-generated LoRA weights in PEFT adapter format.
   225→
   226→    Writes:
   227→    - adapter_model.safetensors: the LoRA weight tensors
   228→    - adapter_config.json: PEFT-compatible configuration
   229→
   230→    Args:
   231→        weights: PEFT state_dict from DocToLoraHypernetwork.forward().
   232→        output_dir: Directory to write adapter files to (created if needed).
   233→        base_model_id: HuggingFace model ID of the base model.
   234→        rank: LoRA rank. Default: 8.
   235→        target_modules: List of module names. Default: ["q_proj", "v_proj"].
   236→
   237→    Note:
   238→        Does NOT include embed_tokens or lm_head — vLLM rejects these in adapters
   239→        (per Phase 21-01 decision: no modules_to_save in LoraConfig).
   240→    """
   241→    from safetensors.torch import save_file  # noqa: PLC0415
   242→
   243→    if target_modules is None:
   244→        target_modules = ["q_proj", "v_proj"]
   245→
   246→    output_path = Path(output_dir)
   247→    output_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   248→
   249→    # Write adapter weights as safetensors
   250→    safetensors_path = output_path / "adapter_model.safetensors"
   251→    save_file(weights, str(safetensors_path))
   252→
   253→    # Write PEFT-compatible adapter_config.json
   254→    adapter_config: dict[str, object] = {
   255→        "peft_type": "LORA",
   256→        "r": rank,
   257→        "lora_alpha": rank * 2,
   258→        "target_modules": target_modules,
   259→        "lora_dropout": 0.0,
   260→        "bias": "none",
   261→        "task_type": "CAUSAL_LM",
   262→        "base_model_name_or_path": base_model_id,
   263→        "inference_mode": True,
   264→        "modules_to_save": None,
   265→        "fan_in_fan_out": False,
   266→    }
   267→
   268→    config_path = output_path / "adapter_config.json"
   269→    config_path.write_text(json.dumps(adapter_config, indent=2))
   270→
```

     1→"""Tests for training-svc endpoint routes."""
     2→
     3→from unittest.mock import patch
     4→
     5→import pytest
     6→
     7→
     8→@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
     9→def clear_job_store():
    10→    """Clear JOB_STORE before/after each test to prevent cross-test contamination."""

    12→
    13→    JOB_STORE.clear()

    17→
    18→def test_train_lora_returns_job_id(test_client):
    19→    """POST /train/lora returns 200 with job_id and status=queued."""

"""Pytest configuration for services/training-svc."""

from typing import Generator

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool
from sqlmodel import SQLModel


@pytest.fixture(scope="function")
def db_engine():
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite:///:memory:",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    SQLModel.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    try:
        yield engine
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.fixture(scope="function")
def db_session(db_engine) -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    session_local = sessionmaker(autocommit=False, autoflush=False, bind=db_engine)
    session = session_local()
    try:
        yield session
    finally:
        session.rollback()
        session.close()


@pytest.fixture(scope="function")
def test_client(db_session) -> Generator[TestClient, None, None]:
    from training_svc.dependencies import get_db
    from training_svc.main import app

    def override_get_db():
        try:
            yield db_session
        finally:
            pass

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_get_db
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        yield client
    app.dependency_overrides.clear()

"""Dependency injection for FastAPI endpoints."""

from collections.abc import Generator

from sqlmodel import Session

from training_svc.storage import engine


def get_db() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    """Provide a database session for dependency injection.

    Yields:
        Database session that automatically commits or rolls back.
    """
    with Session(engine) as session:
        yield session

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/scripts/e2e_smoke.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"End-to-end smoke test for Rune v5.0 milestone.\n\nExercises the full pipeline WITHOUT GPU or network:\n  1. Adapter Registry — CRUD lifecycle\n  2. Inference — provider factory, ABC contracts\n  3. Agent Loop — trajectory persistence, node functions\n  4. Model Training — QLoRA config, hypernetwork forward pass\n  5. Evaluation — pass@k, kill-switch gate, HumanEval subset\n  6. Training Service — HTTP endpoints via TestClient\n\nRun: uv run python scripts/e2e_smoke.py\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nimport uuid\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock, patch\n\npassed = 0\nfailed = 0\n\n\ndef check(name: str, condition: bool, detail: str = \"\") -> None:\n    global passed, failed\n    if condition:\n        passed += 1\n        print(f\"  [PASS] {name}\")\n    else:\n        failed += 1\n        print(f\"  [FAIL] {name} — {detail}\")\n\n\ndef section(title: str) -> None:\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  {title}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n\n# =========================================================================\n#  1. ADAPTER REGISTRY\n# =========================================================================\nsection(\"1. Adapter Registry — CRUD lifecycle\")\n\nfrom sqlalchemy.engine import Engine\nfrom sqlmodel import create_engine\n\nfrom adapter_registry import AdapterRecord, AdapterRegistry\nfrom adapter_registry.exceptions import (\n    AdapterAlreadyExistsError,\n    AdapterNotFoundError,\n)\n\nengine: Engine = create_engine(\"sqlite:///:memory:\")\nregistry = AdapterRegistry(engine=engine)\n\n# Store\nrecord = AdapterRecord(\n    id=\"adapter-001\",\n    version=1,\n    task_type=\"code-gen\",\n    base_model=\"Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct\",\n    adapter_path=\"/tmp/adapters/adapter-001\",\n    file_hash=\"abc123\",\n    file_size=1024,\n    source=\"qlora\",\n    session_id=\"session-001\",\n    is_archived=False,\n)\nregistry.store(record)\ncheck(\"store adapter\", True)\n\n# Retrieve\nretrieved = registry.retrieve_by_id(\"adapter-001\")\ncheck(\"retrieve by id\", retrieved.id == \"adapter-001\")\ncheck(\"retrieve fields intact\", retrieved.task_type == \"code-gen\")\n\n# Duplicate raises\ntry:\n    registry.store(record)\n    check(\"duplicate raises\", False, \"should have raised\")\nexcept AdapterAlreadyExistsError:\n    check(\"duplicate raises AdapterAlreadyExistsError\", True)\n\n# Query by task type\nresults = registry.query_by_task_type(\"code-gen\")\ncheck(\"query by task_type\", len(results) == 1)\ncheck(\"query returns correct record\", results[0].id == \"adapter-001\")\n\n# List all (non-archived)\nall_records = registry.list_all()\ncheck(\"list_all returns non-archived\", len(all_records) == 1)\n\n# Not found raises\ntry:\n    registry.retrieve_by_id(\"nonexistent\")\n    check(\"not found raises\", False, \"should have raised\")\nexcept AdapterNotFoundError:\n    check(\"not found raises AdapterNotFoundError\", True)\n\n\n# =========================================================================\n#  2. INFERENCE PROVIDER ABSTRACTION\n# =========================================================================\nsection(\"2. Inference — provider factory, ABC contracts\")\n\nfrom inference import (\n    GenerationResult,\n    InferenceProvider,\n    OllamaProvider,\n    UnsupportedOperationError,\n    VLLMProvider,\n    get_provider,\n    get_provider_for_step,\n)\n\n# Factory creates correct types\nvllm = get_provider(\"vllm\", base_url=\"http://localhost:8100/v1\")\ncheck(\"get_provider('vllm') returns VLLMProvider\", isinstance(vllm, VLLMProvider))\n\nollama = get_provider(\"ollama\", base_url=\"http://localhost:11434/v1\")\ncheck(\n    \"get_provider('ollama') returns OllamaProvider\",\n    isinstance(ollama, OllamaProvider),\n)\n\n# Caching works\nvllm2 = get_provider(\"vllm\", base_url=\"http://localhost:8100/v1\")\ncheck(\"provider caching (same instance)\", vllm is vllm2)\n\nvllm3 = get_provider(\"vllm\", base_url=\"http://other:8100/v1\")\ncheck(\"different URL gives different instance\", vllm is not vllm3)\n\n# ABC enforcement\ncheck(\"VLLMProvider is InferenceProvider\", isinstance(vllm, InferenceProvider))\ncheck(\"OllamaProvider is InferenceProvider\", isinstance(ollama, InferenceProvider))\n\n# GenerationResult construction\nresult = GenerationResult(\n    text=\"def foo(): pass\",\n    model=\"qwen-7b\",\n    adapter_id=\"adapter-001\",\n    token_count=10,\n    finish_reason=\"stop\",\n)\ncheck(\"GenerationResult fields\", result.text == \"def foo(): pass\")\ncheck(\"GenerationResult adapter_id\", result.adapter_id == \"adapter-001\")\n\n# Invalid provider\ntry:\n    get_provider(\"invalid_provider\")\n    check(\"invalid provider raises\", False)\nexcept ValueError:\n    check(\"invalid provider raises ValueError\", True)\n\n\n# =========================================================================\n#  3. AGENT LOOP — trajectory + nodes\n# =========================================================================\nsection(\"3. Agent Loop — trajectory persistence, node functions\")\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:\n    os.environ[\"RUNE_TRAJECTORY_DIR\"] = tmpdir\n\n    from model_training.trajectory import (\n        format_for_sft,\n        load_trajectory,\n        record_trajectory,\n    )\n\n    # Record a trajectory\n    steps = [\n        {\n            \"code\": \"def add(a, b): return a + b\",\n            \"stdout\": \"\",\n            \"stderr\": \"\",\n            \"exit_code\": 0,\n            \"tests_passed\": True,\n        }\n    ]\n    session_id = \"e2e-test-session\"\n    meta = record_trajectory(\n        session_id=session_id,\n        steps=steps,\n        outcome=\"success\",\n        task_description=\"Add two numbers\",\n        task_type=\"code-gen\",\n        adapter_ids=[\"adapter-001\"],\n    )\n    check(\"record_trajectory returns metadata\", \"path\" in meta)\n    check(\n        \"trajectory file exists\",\n        Path(meta[\"path\"]).exists(),\n    )\n\n    # Load it back\n    loaded = load_trajectory(session_id)\n    check(\"load_trajectory round-trip\", loaded[\"outcome\"] == \"success\")\n    check(\"trajectory has steps\", len(loaded[\"steps\"]) == 1)\n\n    # Format for SFT\n    sft = format_for_sft(loaded)\n    check(\"format_for_sft returns messages\", len(sft) == 3)\n    check(\"SFT has system role\", sft[0][\"role\"] == \"system\")\n    check(\"SFT has user role\", sft[1][\"role\"] == \"user\")\n    check(\"SFT has assistant role\", sft[2][\"role\"] == \"assistant\")\n\n    del os.environ[\"RUNE_TRAJECTORY_DIR\"]\n\n# Node functions (mock inference provider)\nimport asyncio\n\nfrom rune_agent.nodes import (\n    execute_node,\n    generate_node,\n    reflect_node,\n    save_trajectory_node,\n)\n\nmock_provider = AsyncMock()\nmock_provider.generate.return_value = GenerationResult(\n    text='```python\\nprint(\"hello\")\\n```',\n    model=\"test\",\n    adapter_id=None,\n    token_count=5,\n    finish_reason=\"stop\",\n)\n\nwith patch(\"rune_agent.nodes.get_provider\", return_value=mock_provider):\n    state = {\n        \"task_description\": \"Print hello\",\n        \"test_suite\": \"\",\n        \"generated_code\": \"\",\n        \"stdout\": \"\",\n        \"stderr\": \"\",\n        \"exit_code\": -1,\n        \"tests_passed\": False,\n        \"attempt_count\": 0,\n        \"max_attempts\": 3,\n        \"trajectory\": [],\n        \"session_id\": \"e2e-node-test\",\n        \"adapter_ids\": [],\n    }\n    gen_result = asyncio.run(generate_node(state))\n    check(\"generate_node returns generated_code\", \"generated_code\" in gen_result)\n    check(\n        \"generate_node extracts code\",\n        'print(\"hello\")' in gen_result[\"generated_code\"],\n    )\n\n# Execute node — actually runs subprocess\nstate2 = {\n    **state,\n    \"generated_code\": 'print(\"hello world\")',\n    \"test_suite\": \"\",\n}\nexec_result = asyncio.run(execute_node(state2))\ncheck(\"execute_node returns stdout\", \"hello world\" in exec_result[\"stdout\"])\ncheck(\"execute_node exit_code 0\", exec_result[\"exit_code\"] == 0)\ncheck(\"execute_node tests_passed\", exec_result[\"tests_passed\"] is True)\n\n# Reflect node\nstate3 = {**state2, **exec_result, \"attempt_count\": 0, \"trajectory\": []}\nreflect_result = reflect_node(state3)\ncheck(\"reflect_node increments attempt\", reflect_result[\"attempt_count\"] == 1)\ncheck(\"reflect_node appends to trajectory\", len(reflect_result[\"trajectory\"]) == 1)\n\n\n# =========================================================================\n#  4. MODEL TRAINING — config, hypernetwork\n# =========================================================================\nsection(\"4. Model Training — QLoRA config, hypernetwork forward pass\")\n\nfrom model_training.config import get_training_config, validate_config\n\nconfig = get_training_config()\ncheck(\"get_training_config returns dict\", isinstance(config, dict))\ncheck(\"config has rank\", \"rank\" in config)\ncheck(\"config alpha is 2x rank\", config[\"lora_alpha\"] == config[\"rank\"] * 2)\n\nerrors = validate_config(config)\ncheck(\"validate_config on defaults returns no errors\", len(errors) == 0)\n\nbad_config = {**config, \"rank\": 0}\nerrors = validate_config(bad_config)\ncheck(\"validate_config catches bad rank\", len(errors) > 0)\n\n# Hypernetwork (requires torch)\ntry:\n    import torch\n\n    from model_training.hypernetwork import (\n        DocToLoraHypernetwork,\n        save_hypernetwork_adapter,\n    )\n\n    # Small model for CPU test\n    hyper = DocToLoraHypernetwork(\n        input_dim=1000, hidden_dim=32, num_layers=1, rank=4\n    )\n    check(\"DocToLoraHypernetwork instantiates\", True)\n\n    token_ids = torch.randint(0, 1000, (1, 16))\n    weights = hyper(token_ids)\n    check(\"hypernetwork forward returns dict\", isinstance(weights, dict))\n\n    # Check PEFT key format\n    expected_key = (\n        \"base_model.model.model.layers.0.self_attn.q_proj.lora_A.weight\"\n    )\n    check(\"output has PEFT-compatible keys\", expected_key in weights)\n\n    lora_a = weights[expected_key]\n    check(\"lora_A shape is (rank, hidden_dim)\", lora_a.shape == (4, 32))\n\n    # Save adapter\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:\n        save_hypernetwork_adapter(\n            weights=weights,\n            output_dir=tmpdir,\n            base_model_id=\"test-model\",\n            rank=4,\n        )\n        check(\n            \"adapter_model.safetensors written\",\n            (Path(tmpdir) / \"adapter_model.safetensors\").exists(),\n        )\n        check(\n            \"adapter_config.json written\",\n            (Path(tmpdir) / \"adapter_config.json\").exists(),\n        )\n\n        cfg = json.loads((Path(tmpdir) / \"adapter_config.json\").read_text())\n        check(\"adapter config peft_type is LORA\", cfg[\"peft_type\"] == \"LORA\")\n        check(\"adapter config modules_to_save is None\", cfg[\"modules_to_save\"] is None)\n\nexcept ImportError:\n    print(\"  [SKIP] torch not installed — skipping hypernetwork tests\")\n\n\n# =========================================================================\n#  5. EVALUATION — pass@k, kill-switch, HumanEval\n# =========================================================================\nsection(\"5. Evaluation — pass@k, kill-switch gate, HumanEval subset\")\n\nfrom evaluation import calculate_pass_at_k, run_kill_switch_gate\n\n# pass@k\nscore = calculate_pass_at_k(n_samples=10, n_correct=10, k=1)\ncheck(\"pass@k perfect score = 1.0\", score == 1.0)\n\nscore = calculate_pass_at_k(n_samples=10, n_correct=0, k=1)\ncheck(\"pass@k zero score = 0.0\", score == 0.0)\n\nscore = calculate_pass_at_k(n_samples=10, n_correct=5, k=1)\ncheck(\"pass@k partial score in (0, 1)\", 0.0 < score < 1.0)\n\n# Kill-switch gate\nresult = run_kill_switch_gate(\n    adapter_pass1=0.60, baseline_pass1=0.50, threshold=0.05\n)\ncheck(\"kill-switch PASS when adapter > baseline*1.05\", result[\"verdict\"] == \"PASS\")\n\nresult = run_kill_switch_gate(\n    adapter_pass1=0.50, baseline_pass1=0.50, threshold=0.05\n)\ncheck(\"kill-switch FAIL when adapter == baseline\", result[\"verdict\"] == \"FAIL\")\n\n# HumanEval subset — run with canonical solutions\nfrom evaluation.metrics import run_humaneval_subset\n\ndata_path = (\n    Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\n    / \"libs\"\n    / \"evaluation\"\n    / \"src\"\n    / \"evaluation\"\n    / \"data\"\n    / \"humaneval_subset.json\"\n)\nwith data_path.open() as f:\n    tasks = json.load(f)\n\ncheck(\"humaneval_subset.json has 20 tasks\", len(tasks) == 20)\n\n# Run with canonical solutions — should get high pass rate\ncompletions = {t[\"task_id\"]: t[\"canonical_solution\"] for t in tasks[:5]}\nresult = run_humaneval_subset(completions=completions, subset_size=20)\ncheck(\"run_humaneval_subset returns pass_rate\", \"pass_rate\" in result)\ncheck(\n    \"canonical solutions pass rate > 0.5\",\n    result[\"pass_rate\"] > 0.5,\n)\n\n\n# =========================================================================\n#  6. TRAINING SERVICE — HTTP endpoints\n# =========================================================================\nsection(\"6. Training Service — HTTP endpoints via TestClient\")\n\nfrom unittest.mock import patch as mock_patch\n\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\nfrom sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool\nfrom sqlmodel import SQLModel\n\nfrom training_svc.jobs import JOB_STORE\n\nsvc_engine = create_engine(\n    \"sqlite:///:memory:\",\n    connect_args={\"check_same_thread\": False},\n    poolclass=StaticPool,\n)\nSQLModel.metadata.create_all(bind=svc_engine)\n\nwith mock_patch(\"training_svc.storage.engine\", svc_engine):\n    from training_svc.main import app\n\n    with TestClient(app) as client:\n        # POST /train/lora\n        JOB_STORE.clear()\n        with mock_patch(\n            \"training_svc.routers.training._run_training_job\"\n        ):\n            resp = client.post(\n                \"/train/lora\",\n                json={\n                    \"session_id\": \"e2e-session\",\n                    \"task_type\": \"code-gen\",\n                    \"rank\": 8,\n                    \"epochs\": 3,\n                },\n            )\n        check(\"POST /train/lora returns 200\", resp.status_code == 200)\n        data = resp.json()\n        check(\"response has job_id\", \"job_id\" in data)\n        check(\"response status is queued\", data[\"status\"] == \"queued\")\n\n        # GET /jobs/{job_id}\n        job_id = data[\"job_id\"]\n        resp2 = client.get(f\"/jobs/{job_id}\")\n        check(\"GET /jobs/{job_id} returns 200\", resp2.status_code == 200)\n        check(\"job status is queued\", resp2.json()[\"status\"] == \"queued\")\n\n        # GET /jobs/nonexistent\n        resp3 = client.get(\"/jobs/nonexistent\")\n        check(\"GET /jobs/nonexistent returns 404\", resp3.status_code == 404)\n\n        # POST /train/hypernetwork\n        JOB_STORE.clear()\n        with mock_patch(\n            \"training_svc.routers.training._run_hypernetwork_job\"\n        ):\n            resp4 = client.post(\n                \"/train/hypernetwork\",\n                json={\"task_type\": \"gen\", \"trajectory_ids\": [\"t-1\"]},\n            )\n        check(\"POST /train/hypernetwork returns 200\", resp4.status_code == 200)\n        check(\n            \"hypernetwork job status queued\",\n            resp4.json()[\"status\"] == \"queued\",\n        )\n\n        # Poll hypernetwork job\n        hn_job_id = resp4.json()[\"job_id\"]\n        resp5 = client.get(f\"/jobs/{hn_job_id}\")\n        check(\"hypernetwork job pollable\", resp5.status_code == 200)\n\n        # Validation errors\n        resp6 = client.post(\"/train/lora\", json={\"task_type\": \"code-gen\"})\n        check(\n            \"POST /train/lora without session_id returns 422\",\n            resp6.status_code == 422,\n        )\n\n        resp7 = client.post(\n            \"/train/hypernetwork\", json={\"task_type\": \"gen\"}\n        )\n        check(\n            \"POST /train/hypernetwork without trajectory_ids returns 422\",\n            resp7.status_code == 422,\n        )\n\n        # Health check\n        resp8 = client.get(\"/health\")\n        check(\"GET /health returns 200\", resp8.status_code == 200)\n        check(\n            \"health shows training-svc\",\n            resp8.json()[\"service\"] == \"training-svc\",\n        )\n\n\n# =========================================================================\n#  SUMMARY\n# =========================================================================\nprint(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\nprint(f\"  RESULTS: {passed} passed, {failed} failed\")\nprint(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\nif failed > 0:\n    print(\"\\nSome checks failed!\")\n    sys.exit(1)\nelse:\n    print(\"\\nAll checks passed. v5.0 milestone is solid.\")\n    sys.exit(0)\n"
}

```
     1→"""SQLModel table model for LoRA adapter metadata records."""
     2→
     3→from typing import Optional
     4→
     5→from sqlmodel import Field, SQLModel
     6→
     7→
     8→class AdapterRecord(SQLModel, table=True):
     9→    """A stored LoRA adapter metadata record.
    10→
    11→    Tracks metadata for a single LoRA adapter including its lineage,
    12→    storage location, and evaluation metrics. Backed by SQLite via SQLModel.
    13→
    14→    Attributes:
    15→        id: Unique adapter identifier (UUID string).
    16→        version: Adapter version number for lineage tracking.
    17→        task_type: Task category this adapter was trained on (e.g. 'bug-fix').
    18→        base_model_id: Identifier of the base model this adapter was trained from.
    19→        rank: LoRA rank used during training.
    20→        created_at: ISO 8601 timestamp of adapter creation.
    21→        file_path: Filesystem path to the adapter weights file.
    22→        file_hash: SHA-256 hash of the adapter weights file for integrity checks.
    23→        file_size_bytes: Size of the adapter weights file in bytes.
    24→        pass_rate: Pass rate on benchmark tasks (0.0 to 1.0), if evaluated.
    25→        fitness_score: Overall evolutionary fitness score, if evaluated.
    26→        source: How the adapter was created ('distillation', 'evolution', 'manual').
    27→        session_id: ID of the coding session that produced this adapter.
    28→        is_archived: Whether this adapter has been archived (soft delete).
    29→
    30→    Example:
    31→        >>> record = AdapterRecord(
    32→        ...     id="adapter-001", version=1, task_type="bug-fix",
    33→        ...     base_model_id="Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B", rank=16,
    34→        ...     created_at="2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
    35→        ...     file_path="/adapters/adapter-001.safetensors",
    36→        ...     file_hash="abc123", file_size_bytes=1024,
    37→        ...     source="distillation", session_id="sess-001",
    38→        ... )
    39→        >>> record.task_type
    40→        'bug-fix'
    41→        >>> record.is_archived
    42→        False
    43→    """
    44→
    45→    __tablename__ = "adapter_records"
    46→
    47→    id: str = Field(primary_key=True)
    48→    version: int
    49→    task_type: str = Field(index=True)
    50→    base_model_id: str
    51→    rank: int
    52→    created_at: str
    53→    file_path: str
    54→    file_hash: str
    55→    file_size_bytes: int
    56→    pass_rate: Optional[float] = Field(default=None)
    57→    fitness_score: Optional[float] = Field(default=None)
    58→    source: str
    59→    session_id: str
    60→    is_archived: bool = Field(default=False)
    61→
